        Idea:
# Sprint 5: Qwen, Edit-Mode, VLM, and LoRA Preprocessing Parity

## Overall Context

This sprint completes direct image/edit parity and removes WGP-only preprocessing assumptions that would otherwise block orchestrated travel/join/edit-video work.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Do not treat `qwen_image` as equivalent to `qwen_image_2512` unless proven.
- Place Qwen prompt expansion in reigh-worker preprocessing, not template topology.
- Use a LoRA sanitizer and backend-neutral VLM/prompt wrapper with fixture coverage.
- Keep each direct image/edit route WGP-only if its parity is not individually proven.

## Sprint Goal

Finish direct image/edit parity and required preprocessing parity.

## Required Deliverables

- Routes/patches for `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
- Prompt-expander preprocess.
- Backend-neutral VLM/prompt-generation wrapper for travel/join/edit-video callers.
- LoRA sanitizer.
- Checked-in `module_names_<arch>.json`.

## Exit Criteria

Direct image/edit routes are green or individually WGP-only; `qwen_image` is validated before use; prompt expansion, VLM prompt fixtures, and LoRA sanitizer pass fixture corpus.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. {
  "cmd": "RUNPOD_API_KEY=$(awk -F= '/^RUNPOD_API_KEY=/ {sub(/^RUNPOD_API_KEY=/,\"\"); print; exit}' runpod-lifecycle/.env); curl -sS -o /tmp/runpod_ports_u1.json -H \"Authorization: Bearer ${RUNPOD_API_KEY}\" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' --data '{\"query\":\"query { myself { pods { id name desiredStatus runtime { ports { ip publicPort privatePort type } } } } }\"}' https://api.runpod.io/graphql; python3 - <<'PY'\nimport json, subprocess\nfrom pathlib import Path\nj=json.loads(Path('/tmp/runpod_ports_u1.json').read_text())\nports=[]\nfor pod in j.get('data',{}).get('myself',{}).get('pods') or []:\n    if pod.get('desiredStatus') != 'RUNNING':\n        continue\n    for p in (pod.get('runtime') or {}).get('ports') or []:\n        ip=p.get('ip'); pub=p.get('publicPort'); priv=p.get('privatePort')\n        if ip and pub and priv in (7860,8188,8888):\n            ports.append((priv, f'http://{ip}:{pub}'))\nprint('candidate_runtime_http_ports=' + str(len(ports)))\nfor priv,url in ports:\n    probes=['/']\n    if priv==8188: probes=['/object_info','/']\n    elif priv==7860: probes=['/','/queue/status']\n    ok=False\n    statuses=[]\n    for path in probes:\n        cp=subprocess.run(['curl','-sS','--max-time','5','-o','/dev/null','-w','%{http_code}',url+path], capture_output=True, text=True)\n        status=(cp.stdout or '000').strip()[-3:]\n        statuses.append(status)\n        if status and status != '000': ok=True\n    print(f'private_port_{priv}_reachable=' + str(ok).lower() + ' statuses=' + ','.join(statuses))\nPY\nrm -f /tmp/runpod_ports_u1.json",
  "workdir": "/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace",

        Idea:
# Sprint 5: Qwen, Edit-Mode, VLM, and LoRA Preprocessing Parity

## Overall Context

This sprint completes direct image/edit parity and removes WGP-only preprocessing assumptions that would otherwise block orchestrated travel/join/edit-video work.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Do not treat `qwen_image` as equivalent to `qwen_image_2512` unless proven.
- Place Qwen prompt expansion in reigh-worker preprocessing, not template topology.
- Use a LoRA sanitizer and backend-neutral VLM/prompt wrapper with fixture coverage.
- Keep each direct image/edit route WGP-only if its parity is not individually proven.

## Sprint Goal

Finish direct image/edit parity and required preprocessing parity.

## Required Deliverables

- Routes/patches for `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
- Prompt-expander preprocess.
- Backend-neutral VLM/prompt-generation wrapper for travel/join/edit-video callers.
- LoRA sanitizer.
- Checked-in `module_names_<arch>.json`.

## Exit Criteria

Direct image/edit routes are green or individually WGP-only; `qwen_image` is validated before use; prompt expansion, VLM prompt fixtures, and LoRA sanitizer pass fixture corpus.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Idea:
# Sprint 5: Qwen, Edit-Mode, VLM, and LoRA Preprocessing Parity

## Overall Context

This sprint completes direct image/edit parity and removes WGP-only preprocessing assumptions that would otherwise block orchestrated travel/join/edit-video work.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Do not treat `qwen_image` as equivalent to `qwen_image_2512` unless proven.
- Place Qwen prompt expansion in reigh-worker preprocessing, not template topology.
- Use a LoRA sanitizer and backend-neutral VLM/prompt wrapper with fixture coverage.
- Keep each direct image/edit route WGP-only if its parity is not individually proven.

## Sprint Goal

Finish direct image/edit parity and required preprocessing parity.

## Required Deliverables

- Routes/patches for `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
- Prompt-expander preprocess.
- Backend-neutral VLM/prompt-generation wrapper for travel/join/edit-video callers.
- LoRA sanitizer.
- Checked-in `module_names_<arch>.json`.

## Exit Criteria

Direct image/edit routes are green or individually WGP-only; `qwen_image` is validated before use; prompt expansion, VLM prompt fixtures, and LoRA sanitizer pass fixture corpus.

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. test_vlm_transition():\n+    \"\"\"Test function for VLM transition prompt generation.\"\"\"\n+    headless_logger.essential(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)\n+    headless_logger.essential(\"Testing VLM Transition Prompt Generation\")\n+    headless_logger.essential(\"=\" * 80 + \"\\n\")\n+\n+    headless_logger.essential(\"To test, call:\")\n+    headless_logger.essential(\"  generate_transition_prompt('path/to/start.jpg', 'path/to/end.jpg')\")\n+    headless_logger.essential(\"\\nExample usage in travel orchestrator:\")\n+    headless_logger.essential(\"  if orchestrator_payload.get('enhance_prompt', False):\")\n+    headless_logger.essential(\"      prompt = generate_transition_prompt(start_img, end_img, base_prompt)\")\n+\n+\n+if __name__ == \"__main__\":\n+    test_vlm_transition()\n*** End Patch\n"

validation.\"\"\"\n+\n+from __future__ import annotations\n+\n+import json\n+from dataclasses import dataclass\n+from pathlib import Path\n+from typing import Iterable\n+\n+\n+MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1\n+MANIFEST_DIR = Path(__file__).with_name(\"module_manifests\")\n+\n+\n+class LoRAModuleManifestError(ValueError):\n+    \"\"\"Raised when a LoRA module manifest is missing or malformed.\"\"\"\n+\n+\n+@dataclass(frozen=True)\n+class LoRAModuleManifest:\n+    \"\"\"Validated LoRA module names for one architecture.\"\"\"\n+\n+    architecture: str\n+    module_names: tuple[str, ...]\n+    source_path: Path\n+    schema_version: int = MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION\n+\n+\n+def manifest_filename_for_architecture(architecture: str) -> str:\n+    if not architecture or not architecture.strip():\n+        raise LoRAModuleManifestError(\"architecture is required\")\n+    return f\"module_names_{architecture}.json\"\n+\n+\n+def manifest_path_for_architecture(\n+    architecture: str,\n+    manifest_dir: Path | None = None,\n+) -> Path:\n+    directory = manifest_dir or MANIFEST_DIR\n+    return directory / manifest_filename_for_architecture(architecture)\n+\n+\n+def architecture_from_manifest_filename(path: Path) -> str:\n+    name = path.name\n+    if not name.startswith(\"module_names_\") or not name.endswith(\".json\"):\n+        raise LoRAModuleManifestError(f\"invalid manifest filename: {name}\")\n+    return name.removeprefix(\"module_names_\").removesuffix(\".json\")\n+\n+\n+def _validate_module_names(value: object, *, path: Path) -> tuple[str, ...]:\n+    if not isinstance(value, list) or not value:\n+        raise LoRAModuleManifestError(f\"{path}: module_names must be a non-empty list\")\n+\n+    names: list[str] = []\n+    seen: set[str] = set()\n+    for item in value:\n+        if not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip():\n+            raise LoRAModuleManifestError(f\"{path}: module_names entries must be non-empty strings\")\n+        name = item.strip()\n+        if name in seen:\n+            raise LoRAModuleManifestError(f\"{path}: duplicate module name '{name}'\")\n+        names.append(name)\n+        seen.add(name)\n+    return tuple(names)\n+\n+\n+def parse_module_manifest(data: object, *, source_path: Path) -> LoRAModuleManifest:\n+    if not isinstance(data, dict):\n+        raise LoRAModuleManifestError(f\"{source_path}: manifest must be a JSON object\")\n+\n+    schema_version = data.get(\"schema_version\")\n+    if schema_version != MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION:\n+        raise LoRAModuleManifestError(\n+            f\"{source_path}: schema_version must be {MANIFEST_SCHEMA_VERSION}\"\n+        )\n+\n+    architecture = data.get(\"architecture\")\n+    if not isinstance(architecture, str) or not architecture.strip():\n+        raise LoRAModuleManifestError(f\"{source_path}: architecture must be a non-empty string\")\n+    architecture = architecture.strip()\n+\n+    expected_architecture = architecture_from_manifest_filename(source_path)\n+    if architecture != expected_architecture:\n+        raise LoRAModuleManifestError(\n+            f\"{source_path}: architecture '{architecture}' does not match filename \"\n+            f\"'{expected_architecture}'\"\n+        )\n+\n+    return LoRAModuleManifest(\n+        architecture=architecture,\n+        module_names=_validate_module_names(data.get(\"module_names\"), path=source_path),\n+        source_path=source_path,\n+        schema_version=schema_version,\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def load_module_manifest(\n+    architecture: str,\n+    manifest_dir: Path | None = None,\n+) -> LoRAModuleManifest:\n+    path = manifest_path_for_architecture(architecture, manifest_dir)\n+    if not path.is_file():\n+        raise LoRAModuleManifestError(f\"missing LoRA module manifest for architecture '{architecture}'\")\n+    try:\n+        data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n+    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n+        raise LoRAModuleManifestError(f\"{path}: invalid JSON: {exc}\") from exc\n+    return parse_module_manifest(data, source_path=path)\n+\n+\n+def iter_module_manifest_paths(manifest_dir: Path | None = None) -> Iterable[Path]:\n+    directory = manifest_dir or MANIFEST_DIR\n+    if not directory.is_dir():\n+        return ()\n+    return tuple(sorted(directory.glob(\"module_names_*.json\")))\n+\n+\n+def load_all_module_manifests(\n+    manifest_dir: Path | None = None,\n+) -> dict[str, LoRAModuleManifest]:\n+    manifests: dict[str, LoRAModuleManifest] = {}\n+    for path in iter_module_manifest_paths(manifest_dir):\n+        manifest = parse_module_manifest(\n+            json.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")),\n+            source_path=path,\n+        )\n+        manifests[manifest.architecture] = manifest\n+    return manifests\n*** End Patch\n"
}

        Idea:
# Sprint 5: Qwen, Edit-Mode, VLM, and LoRA Preprocessing Parity

## Overall Context

This sprint completes direct image/edit parity and removes WGP-only preprocessing assumptions that would otherwise block orchestrated travel/join/edit-video work.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Do not treat `qwen_image` as equivalent to `qwen_image_2512` unless proven.
- Place Qwen prompt expansion in reigh-worker preprocessing, not template topology.
- Use a LoRA sanitizer and backend-neutral VLM/prompt wrapper with fixture coverage.
- Keep each direct image/edit route WGP-only if its parity is not individually proven.

## Sprint Goal

Finish direct image/edit parity and required preprocessing parity.

## Required Deliverables

- Routes/patches for `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
- Prompt-expander preprocess.
- Backend-neutral VLM/prompt-generation wrapper for travel/join/edit-video callers.
- LoRA sanitizer.
- Checked-in `module_names_<arch>.json`.

## Exit Criteria

Direct image/edit routes are green or individually WGP-only; `qwen_image` is validated before use; prompt expansion, VLM prompt fixtures, and LoRA sanitizer pass fixture corpus.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

value.strip().lower() in {\"1\", \"true\", \"yes\", \"on\"}\n+    return bool(value)\n+\n+\n+def expand_qwen_prompt(\n+    prompt: str,\n+    *,\n+    task_type: str,\n+    params: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,\n+    expander_factory: Callable[..., Any] = create_qwen_prompt_expander,\n+    model_name: str | None = None,\n+    device: str | int = \"cpu\",\n+    seed: int = -1,\n+) -> PromptExpansionResult:\n+    \"\"\"Expand a Qwen prompt when explicitly requested by worker params.\"\"\"\n+    original_prompt = prompt or \"\"\n+    requested = qwen_prompt_expansion_requested(params)\n+\n+    if not requested:\n+        return _unchanged(original_prompt, task_type=task_type, requested=False, reason=\"not_requested\")\n+    if task_type not in QWEN_PROMPT_EXPANSION_TASK_TYPES:\n+        return _unchanged(original_prompt, task_type=task_type, requested=True, reason=\"unsupported_task_type\")\n+    if not original_prompt.strip():\n+        return _unchanged(original_prompt, task_type=task_type, requested=True, reason=\"empty_prompt\")\n+\n+    selected_model = model_name or str((params or {}).get(\"qwen_prompt_expansion_model\") or \"Qwen2.5_14B\")\n+    expander = expander_factory(model_name=selected_model, device=device, is_vl=False)\n+    result = expander.extend(\n+        prompt=original_prompt,\n+        system_prompt=QWEN_PROMPT_EXPANSION_SYSTEM_PROMPT,\n+        seed=seed,\n+    )\n+    expanded_prompt = str(getattr(result, \"prompt\", \"\") or \"\").strip()\n+    if not expanded_prompt:\n+        return _unchanged(original_prompt, task_type=task_type, requested=True, reason=\"empty_expansion\")\n+\n+    return PromptExpansionResult(\n+        prompt=expanded_prompt,\n+        metadata=PromptExpansionMetadata(\n+            provider=\"qwen\",\n+            task_type=task_type,\n+            requested=True,\n+            applied=True,\n+            model_name=selected_model,\n+            original_prompt=original_prompt,\n+            expanded_prompt=expanded_prompt,\n+            raw={\n+                \"status\": getattr(result, \"status\", None),\n+                \"seed\": getattr(result, \"seed\", None),\n+                \"message\": getattr(result, \"message\", None),\n+            },\n+        ),\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def _unchanged(\n+    prompt: str,\n+    *,\n+    task_type: str,\n+    requested: bool,\n+    reason: str,\n+) -> PromptExpansionResult:\n+    return PromptExpansionResult(\n+        prompt=prompt,\n+ == []\n+    assert wan_with_ltx.loras == []\n+    assert ltx_with_qwen.loras == []\n+    assert qwen_with_wan.decisions[0].reason == \"architecture_mismatch:wan_lora_for_qwen_model\"\n+    assert wan_with_ltx.decisions[0].reason == \"architecture_mismatch:ltx_lora_for_wan_model\"\n+    assert ltx_with_qwen.decisions[0].reason == \"architecture_mismatch:qwen_lora_for_ltx_model\"\n+\n+\n+def test_missing_manifest_fails_for_architecture_scoped_loras():\n+    with pytest.raises(LoRAModuleManifestError, match=\"missing LoRA module manifest\"):\n+        sanitize_lora_values(\n+            [\"https://huggingface.co/acme/qwen_style.safetensors\"],\n+            [1.0],\n+            architecture=\"qwen_missing_20B\",\n+        )\n+\n+\n+def test_payload_sanitizer_preserves_phase_multipliers():\n+    result = sanitize_lora_payload(\n+        {\n+            \"activated_loras\": [\n+                \"https://huggingface.co/acme/qwen_high.safetensors\",\n+                \"https://huggingface.co/acme/qwen_low.safetensors\",\n+            ],\n+            \"loras_multipliers\": \"1;0 0;1\",\n+        },\n+        architecture=QWEN_ARCH,\n+    )\n+\n+    assert result.loras == [\n+        \"https://huggingface.co/acme/qwen_high.safetensors\",\n+        \"https://huggingface.co/acme/qwen_low.safetensors\",\n+    ]\n+    assert result.multipliers == [\"1;0\", \"0;1\"]\n+\n+\n+def test_lora_config_parsing_uses_sanitizer_before_payload_construction():\n+    params = {\n+        \"activated_loras\": [\n+            \"https://huggingface.co/acme/qwen-style/blob/main/qwen_style.safetensors?download=1\",\n+            \"https://huggingface.co/acme/qwen-style/resolve/main/qwen_style.safetensors\",\n+            \"https://huggingface.co/acme/wan2.2_i2v_lightx2v_4steps_lora.safetensors\",\n+        ],\n+        \"loras_multipliers\": \"0.7 0.9 1.0\",\n+    }\n+\n+    config = LoRAConfig.from_params(params, model=QWEN_ARCH, task_id=\"sanitize\")\n+\n+    assert len(config.entries) == 1\n+    assert config.entries[0].url == (\n+        \"https://huggingface.co/acme/qwen-style/resolve/main/qwen_style.safetensors\"\n+    )\n+    assert config.entries[0].multiplier == 0.7\n+\n+\n+def test_phase_config_parser_uses_manifest_backed_sanitizer():\n+    phase_config = {\n+        \"num_phases\": 2,\n+        \"steps_per_phase\": [2, 2],\n+        \"phases\": [\n+            {\n+ {\"url\": \"https://huggingface.co/acme/ltx_ic-lora.safetensors\", \"multiplier\": 0.5},\n+                ],\n+            },\n+            {\n+                \"guidance_scale\": 1.0,\n+ \"https://huggingface.co/acme/ltx_ic-lora.safetensors\", \"multiplier\": 0.5},\n+                ],\n+            },\n+        ],\n+    }\n+\n+    parsed = parse_phase_config(\n+         Idea:
# Sprint 5: Qwen, Edit-Mode, VLM, and LoRA Preprocessing Parity

## Overall Context

This sprint completes direct image/edit parity and removes WGP-only preprocessing assumptions that would otherwise block orchestrated travel/join/edit-video work.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Do not treat `qwen_image` as equivalent to `qwen_image_2512` unless proven.
- Place Qwen prompt expansion in reigh-worker preprocessing, not template topology.
- Use a LoRA sanitizer and backend-neutral VLM/prompt wrapper with fixture coverage.
- Keep each direct image/edit route WGP-only if its parity is not individually proven.

## Sprint Goal

Finish direct image/edit parity and required preprocessing parity.

## Required Deliverables

- Routes/patches for `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
- Prompt-expander preprocess.
- Backend-neutral VLM/prompt-generation wrapper for travel/join/edit-video callers.
- LoRA sanitizer.
- Checked-in `module_names_<arch>.json`.

## Exit Criteria

Direct image/edit routes are green or individually WGP-only; `qwen_image` is validated before use; prompt expansion, VLM prompt fixtures, and LoRA sanitizer pass fixture corpus.

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the path-mapping defect from the previous iteration is fixed, but a different technical gap remains around `self_refiner`. repository search shows `shared.utils.self_refiner` is imported by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/distilled.py`, `ti2vid_one_stage.py`, `ti2vid_two_stages.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/helpers.py`, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`; the v4 plan still aligns `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` to upstream head without any concrete runtime step that exercises those code paths after the change, so the only repo-documented behavior check for that file is still missing from the executable body. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the direct adapter still has a correctness risk around resolution parity. current vibecomfy ready templates hardcode image dimensions (`image/z_image` uses `emptysd3latentimage` 1024x1024 and `image/qwen_image_2512` uses 768x768), while `vibecomfy run` exposes no `--resolution` flag; the revised plan says a temporary scratchpad/input file may be generated only if supported, otherwise resolution is left out. that means a vibecomfy-supported direct route can silently ignore the worker's `resolution` param unless scratchpad resolution patching is made mandatory for supported direct routes or the support is explicitly limited to default-resolution smoke fixtures. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-success-criteria-well-prioritized-and-verifiable: are the success criteria well-prioritized and verifiable?: the attached success criteria are stale and do not match the current plan body. they still require 'no `usestate` for `activepairdata`', '`setactivepairdata` fully removed', 'sync effect deleted', and '`onpairclick` simplified to `(pairindex: number) => void`', which are the opposite of the current targeted-sync plan. as presented, the criteria are not usable for review or execution. # Sprint 5: Qwen, Edit-Mode, VLM, and LoRA Preprocessing Parity

## Overall Context

This sprint completes direct image/edit parity and removes WGP-only preprocessing assumptions that would otherwise block orchestrated travel/join/edit-video work.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Do not treat `qwen_image` as equivalent to `qwen_image_2512` unless proven.
- Place Qwen prompt expansion in reigh-worker preprocessing, not template topology.
- Use a LoRA sanitizer and backend-neutral VLM/prompt wrapper with fixture coverage.
- Keep each direct image/edit route WGP-only if its parity is not individually proven.

## Sprint Goal

Finish direct image/edit parity and required preprocessing parity.

## Required Deliverables

- Routes/patches for `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
- Prompt-expander preprocess.
- Backend-neutral VLM/prompt-generation wrapper for travel/join/edit-video callers.
- LoRA sanitizer.
- Checked-in `module_names_<arch>.json`.

## Exit Criteria

Direct image/edit routes are green or individually WGP-only; `qwen_image` is validated before use; prompt expansion, VLM prompt fixtures, and LoRA sanitizer pass fixture corpus.

- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies — even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

        Idea:
# Sprint 5: Qwen, Edit-Mode, VLM, and LoRA Preprocessing Parity

## Overall Context

This sprint completes direct image/edit parity and removes WGP-only preprocessing assumptions that would otherwise block orchestrated travel/join/edit-video work.

## Shared Operating Rules

- Do not treat `qwen_image` as equivalent to `qwen_image_2512` unless proven.
- Place Qwen prompt expansion in reigh-worker preprocessing, not template topology.
- Use a LoRA sanitizer and backend-neutral VLM/prompt wrapper with fixture coverage.
- Keep each direct image/edit route WGP-only if its parity is not individually proven.

## Sprint Goal

Finish direct image/edit parity and required preprocessing parity.

## Required Deliverables

- Routes/patches for `qwen_image_2512`, `qwen_image`, `qwen_image_edit`, `qwen_image_style`, `image_inpaint`, and `annotated_image_edit`.
- Prompt-expander preprocess.
- Backend-neutral VLM/prompt-generation wrapper for travel/join/edit-video callers.
- LoRA sanitizer.
- Checked-in `module_names_<arch>.json`.

## Exit Criteria

Direct image/edit routes are green or individually WGP-only; `qwen_image` is validated before use; prompt expansion, VLM prompt fixtures, and LoRA sanitizer pass fixture corpus.
